📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期麻疹病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 在153名受访者中,139名(91%)表示其机构要求某些医疗人员提供麻疹免疫力的证据;20%的机构将其要求应用于特定群体。仅有36%的机构以可访问的电子格式存储雇员的免疫力数据。暴露定义存在差异;33%的受访者使用了可能低估暴露风险的标准(例如,共享空气超过15分钟)。其他机构缺乏获取空气交换数据的途径(33%)。此外,12%至31%的受访者表示其机构不一致地推荐对非免疫个体进行暴露后预防。
  • 研究发现麻疹病毒多次引入犹他州,其中一次引入占大多数病例。随着麻疹传播,出现了两个系统发育分支,其地理病例构成不同。研究识别出共享学校与基因组聚类之间存在显著关联,并重建了校内传播链及源自校际体育赛事的传播链。
  • 即时预测估计值提高了累计暴发规模估计值的准确性,相比临时数据更为准确,且Rt估计值总体反映了潜在的发病趋势。然而,1月份因临时数据不完整,估计值准确性下降。 [10.15585/mmwr.mm7533a1]。
  • 综述得出结论:在中心假设下,仅依靠常规MCV1覆盖在数学上不足以达到群体免疫阈值。然而,高完整两剂系列覆盖率理论上仍有可能达到该阈值,但操作上具有挑战性。监测数据显示与Rcov存在正序地理一致性,表明覆盖率与暴发风险之间可能存在联系。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Of 153 respondents, 139 (91%) reported that their institutions required evidence of measles immunity for some healthcare personnel; 20% applied this requirement to a subset.
  • We found multiple introductions of measles into Utah, with one accounting for the majority of cases. As measles spread, two phylogenetic clades emerged with differing geographical case compositions.
  • The authors reported that measles has a high transmissibility rate (R 0 of 12-18) and can spread rapidly among susceptible individuals.
  • Nowcast estimates improved the accuracy of cumulative outbreak size compared with provisional data, and Rt estimates generally reflected underlying transmission trends.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Infection Control & Hospital Epidemiology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-26  |  DOI: 10.1017/ice.2026.10498  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 1-6

麻疹预防与控制在急性医疗环境中的应用——关注差距

Measles prevention and control in the acute healthcare setting—minding the gaps
作者： Loreen A. Herwaldt, Hilary M. Babcock, Karen B. Brust, Susan E. Beekmann, PhilipM. Polgreen
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景:随着美国麻疹 疫苗接种发病率下降而发病率上升,医疗机构面临更高的麻疹 传播风险。在临床环境中发生麻疹暴露会导致广泛的接触追踪调查、显著的人力资源中断、高昂的成本以及易感患者潜在感染。本研究旨在评估美国医院识别和管理麻疹暴露的准备情况,并找出常见的感染预防差距。方法:2025年,新兴感染网络(EIN)对其医生成员进行了9个问题的电子问卷调查。问题涉及免疫接种政策、暴露识别能力以及管理协议。结果:在153名受访者中,其执业范围包括麻疹预防,其中139名(91%)表示其机构要求某些医疗人员提供麻疹免疫力的证据;20%的机构将其要求应用于特定群体。仅有36%的机构以可访问的电子格式存储雇员的免疫力数据。大多数机构能够确定患者是否处于特定临床区域,但无法识别访客或非雇员人员。暴露定义存在差异;33%的受访者使用了可能低估暴露风险的标准(例如,共享空气超过15分钟)。其他机构缺乏获取空气交换数据的途径(33%)。此外,12%至31%的受访者表示其机构不一致地推荐对非免疫个体进行暴露后预防。结论:尽管麻疹具有高度传染性,但美国医院在记录保存、暴露定义和预防协议方面仍存在显著差距。标准化的暴露标准以及改善对员工免疫力记录的可访问性有助于减少医疗环境中麻疹 传播及其相关的临床和财务不良后果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
随着美国麻疹 疫苗接种发病率下降而发病率上升,医疗机构面临更高的麻疹 传播风险。本研究旨在评估美国医院识别和管理麻疹暴露的准备情况,并找出常见的感染预防差距。
设计与对象
2025年,新兴感染网络(EIN)对其医生成员进行了9个问题的电子问卷调查。在153名受访者中,其执业范围包括麻疹预防,其中139名(91%)表示其机构要求某些医疗人员提供麻疹免疫力的证据;20%的机构将其要求应用于特定群体。
核心方法
本研究采用电子问卷评估免疫接种政策、暴露识别能力和管理协议。问题涉及麻疹预防实践,包括对免疫力记录的要求和暴露定义。
主要结果
在153名受访者中,139名(91%)表示其机构要求某些医疗人员提供麻疹免疫力的证据;20%的机构将其要求应用于特定群体。仅有36%的机构以可访问的电子格式存储雇员的免疫力数据。暴露定义存在差异;33%的受访者使用了可能低估暴露风险的标准(例如,共享空气超过15分钟)。其他机构缺乏获取空气交换数据的途径(33%)。此外,12%至31%的受访者表示其机构不一致地推荐对非免疫个体进行暴露后预防。
解释与创新
作者认为,尽管麻疹具有高度传染性,但美国医院在记录保存、暴露定义和预防协议方面仍存在显著差距。本研究强调了制定标准化暴露标准以及改善对员工免疫力记录的可访问性的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为改善医疗环境中麻疹的预防与控制提供依据,通过解决免疫力记录、暴露定义和暴露后预防协议方面的差距。这些改进有助于减少麻疹 传播及其相关的临床和财务不良后果。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于依赖于医生成员的自我报告数据,这可能引入偏差。由于回答可能存在差异且缺乏直接的观察数据,证据强度中等。

Measles prevention and control in the acute healthcare setting—minding the gaps

Authors: Loreen A. Herwaldt, Hilary M. Babcock, Karen B. Brust, Susan E. Beekmann, PhilipM. Polgreen
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: As measles vaccination rates in the United States decline and incidence rises, healthcare facilities face an increased risk of measles transmission. Measles exposures in clinical settings lead to extensive contact tracing investigations, significant staffing disruptions, high costs, and potential infections in vulnerable patients. This study aimed to assess US hospitals’ readiness of to identify and manage measles exposures and to identify common infection prevention gaps. Methods: In 2025, the Emerging Infections Network (EIN) conducted a 9-question electronic survey of its physician members. The questions addressed immunization policies, exposure identification capabilities, and management protocols. Results: Of 153 respondents whose scope of practice includes measles prevention, 139 (91%) reported that their institutions required evidence of measles immunity for some healthcare personnel; 20% applied this requirement to a subset. Only 36% of facilities stored immunity data for employed staff in accessible electronic format. Most facilities could determine when patients were in specific clinical areas but could not identify visitors or non-employed staff. Exposure definitions varied; 33% of respondents used criteria that potentially underestimate exposure risk (eg, >15 min of shared air). Others lacked access to air exchange data (33%). Furthermore, 12%–31% of respondents reported their institutions do not consistently recommend postexposure prophylaxis for non-immune individuals. Conclusions: Despite the high transmissibility of measles, significant gaps in record-keeping, exposure definitions, and prophylaxis protocols persist in US hospitals. Standardized exposure criteria and improved accessibility to staff immunity records could help reduce measles transmission in health care and the associated clinical and financial adverse outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
As measles vaccination rates in the United States decline and incidence rises, healthcare facilities face an increased risk of measles transmission. This study aimed to assess US hospitals’ readiness to identify and manage measles exposures and to identify common infection prevention gaps.
Design and population
In 2025, the Emerging Infections Network (EIN) conducted a 9-question electronic survey of its physician members. Of 153 respondents whose scope of practice includes measles prevention, 139 (91%) reported that their institutions required evidence of measles immunity for some healthcare personnel; 20% applied this requirement to a subset.
Core methods
The study used an electronic survey to assess immunization policies, exposure identification capabilities, and management protocols. The questions addressed measles prevention practices, including requirements for immunity documentation and exposure definitions.
Main results
Of 153 respondents, 139 (91%) reported that their institutions required evidence of measles immunity for some healthcare personnel; 20% applied this requirement to a subset. Only 36% of facilities stored immunity data for employed staff in accessible electronic format. Exposure definitions varied; 33% of respondents used criteria that potentially underestimate exposure risk (eg, >15 min of shared air). Others lacked access to air exchange data (33%). Furthermore, 12%–31% of respondents reported their institutions do not consistently recommend postexposure prophylaxis for non-immune individuals.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that despite the high transmissibility of measles, significant gaps in record-keeping, exposure definitions, and prophylaxis protocols persist in US hospitals. The study highlights the need for standardized exposure criteria and improved accessibility to staff immunity records.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform efforts to improve measles prevention and control in healthcare settings by addressing gaps in immunity documentation, exposure definitions, and postexposure prophylaxis protocols. These improvements could help reduce measles transmission and its associated clinical and financial adverse outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data from a survey of physician members, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the potential for variability in responses and the lack of direct observational data.

分层基因组监测能否弥合孟加拉国麻疹分子流行病学的缺口?

Can Tiered Genomic Surveillance Close the Gaps in Measles Molecular Epidemiology?
作者： P. Kanta, K. Chhikara, Kapil Goyal, A. Ghosh, Mini P. Singh
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过评估分层基因组监测在识别孟加拉国麻疹 暴发影响因素中的效果,以弥合麻疹分子流行病学的缺口。作者强调,麻疹的消除需依赖完善的监测系统、快速的暴发检测及迅速的公共卫生干预。
设计与对象
本研究聚焦孟加拉国麻疹 暴发,分析分层基因组监测在疾病趋势监测与传播模式识别中的作用。研究纳入了近期暴发数据,并强调了监测在支持麻疹消除目标中的重要性。
核心方法
作者回顾了麻疹 暴发数据,并采用分层基因组监测技术监测疾病趋势与传播模式。研究分析了导致暴发的因素,并探讨了监测在公共卫生干预中的作用。
主要结果
The authors reported that measles has a high transmissibility rate (R 0 of 12-18) and can spread rapidly among susceptible individuals. They highlighted the need for improved surveillance systems to detect and respond to outbreaks effectively.
解释与创新
作者将其发现解释为分层基因组监测可增进对麻疹 传播动态的理解,并支持消除工作。研究强调将监测与暴发应对策略相结合的创新性。
科研与公卫意义
研究发现表明,分层基因组监测可为更有针对性的公共卫生干预提供信息,并提升暴发检测能力,这对麻疹消除至关重要。研究还强调监测疾病趋势以预防易感人群中的快速传播的重要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告本研究的具体局限性,但其基于暴发数据的回顾,并以综述性评论而非详细分析形式呈现。由于缺乏具体统计或方法学细节,证据强度为中等。

Can Tiered Genomic Surveillance Close the Gaps in Measles Molecular Epidemiology?

Authors: P. Kanta, K. Chhikara, Kapil Goyal, A. Ghosh, Mini P. Singh
Original abstract (English)
in their recent communication [1] have highlighted the possible factors responsible for the measles outbreak in Bangladesh.The authors have emphasised that measles elimination depends on robust surveillance systems, prompt outbreak detection, and rapid public health interventions.Monitoring of disease trends and timely identification of transmission patterns are pertinent to support measles elimination goals [2].The measles virus has a high transmissibility rate (R 0 of 12-18) [3] and the infection can spread rapidly in susceptible subjects.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the gaps in measles molecular epidemiology by evaluating the effectiveness of tiered genomic surveillance in identifying factors contributing to measles outbreaks, particularly in Bangladesh. The authors emphasized that measles elimination requires robust surveillance systems, prompt outbreak detection, and rapid public health interventions.
Design and population
The study focused on measles outbreaks in Bangladesh, analyzing the role of genomic surveillance in monitoring disease trends and identifying transmission patterns. It included data from recent outbreaks and highlighted the importance of surveillance in supporting measles elimination goals.
Core methods
The authors reviewed measles outbreak data and utilized genomic surveillance techniques to monitor disease trends and transmission patterns. They analyzed the factors contributing to the outbreaks and discussed the role of surveillance in public health interventions.
Main results
The authors reported that measles has a high transmissibility rate (R 0 of 12-18) and can spread rapidly among susceptible individuals. They highlighted the need for improved surveillance systems to detect and respond to outbreaks effectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as evidence that tiered genomic surveillance can enhance the understanding of measles transmission dynamics and support elimination efforts. They emphasized the novelty of integrating surveillance with outbreak response strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that genomic surveillance can inform more targeted public health interventions and improve outbreak detection, which is critical for measles elimination. They also highlight the importance of monitoring disease trends to prevent rapid spread in susceptible populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study, but the findings are based on a review of outbreak data and are presented as a general commentary rather than a detailed analysis. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of specific statistical or methodological details.

美国学校中麻疹 疫苗的稳健分配需对未测量的引入风险进行对冲

Robust measles vaccine allocation in US schools requires hedging against unmeasured introduction risk
作者： Laura W. Alexander, Aakash Pandey, Nathaniel Hupert, Emily A. Serman, Lior Rennert, Ana I. Bento, Alexander, L. W., Serman, E. A., Bento, A. I.
摘要中文翻译摘要 美国正濒临失去麻疹消除状态的边缘,而可预防该情况的疫苗剂次已被承诺使用;仍有待确定的是哪些学校应优先获得这些疫苗。分配理论通过按边际群体免疫回报而非最低覆盖率对学校进行排序来回答这一问题。在36,031所美国学校中,该定理的约束性情况为常见情况:三分之二至四分之三的易感幼儿园儿童就读于学校,其中每增加一剂疫苗可带来递增的群体免疫。该排序可带来1.92倍的间接保护(相较于最低覆盖率优先),或1.16倍的总体保护。但该策略假设所有学校感染病例的可能性均等。2025-26学年南卡罗来纳州北部暴发的既往记录与该前提不符:在易感人数最高的十分位组中,暴露学校的比例过高,达5.0倍(95%置信区间3.0至7.3),而作为阴性对照的在校人数(仅反映学校规模而非易感性)则无此现象。华盛顿州克拉克县的一项独立暴发也再现了该缩放关系,但全美仅有两个司法管辖区公布的记录允许进行此类检验,因此风险缩放的陡峭程度在全国范围内仍未明确。在此不确定性下,理论上最优的分配规则是我们评估的七种规则中最不稳健的一种,在最坏情况下损失了86%的可达收益,且无论不确定性集合如何构建,该脆弱性均成立。对已测量风险进行轻度对冲可在主要预算下保留约90%的收益,该结果基于已公布两列数据的州份计算。在针对测量良好的变量进行目标优化而暴露风险未被测量的情况下,点估计最优解即为脆弱选择。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决美国学校中麻疹 疫苗分配的稳健性需求,以防止失去麻疹消除状态,重点关注应优先向哪些学校分配疫苗以最大化群体免疫。
设计与对象
本研究在36,031所美国学校中评估疫苗分配策略,考虑麻疹消除状态及疫苗分配对群体免疫的潜在影响,特别关注易感幼儿园儿童数量较多的学校。
核心方法
本研究方法涉及应用分配理论,基于边际群体免疫回报对学校进行排序,并利用特定暴发的既往记录评估学校易感性与暴发风险之间的关系。
主要结果
结果表明,按边际群体免疫回报对学校进行排序可带来1.92倍的间接保护(相较于最低覆盖率优先),以及1.16倍的总体保护。然而,所有学校感染病例可能性均等的假设受到暴发数据挑战:易感人数最高的十分位组中,暴露学校的比例过高,达5.0倍。
解释与创新
作者解读认为,在风险缩放不确定性下,理论上最优的分配规则具有脆弱性,而对已测量风险进行轻度对冲可保留主要预算约90%的收益。这强调了在疫苗分配策略中考虑未测量引入风险的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为更精准的风险评估在麻疹 疫苗分配中的必要性提供了依据,强调在目标学校时应兼顾高易感性并考虑未测量引入风险,以改善公共卫生结局。
局限与证据强度
证据受限于风险缩放的全国性数据缺失,因全美仅有两个司法管辖区公布的记录允许进行此类检验。这导致研究结果的置信度为中等,因最优规则的脆弱性在不确定性下仍未明确。

Robust measles vaccine allocation in US schools requires hedging against unmeasured introduction risk

Authors: Laura W. Alexander, Aakash Pandey, Nathaniel Hupert, Emily A. Serman, Lior Rennert, Ana I. Bento, Alexander, L. W., Serman, E. A., Bento, A. I.
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The United States is on the verge of losing measles elimination status, and the doses that could prevent it are already committed; what is still open is which schools get them first. Allocation theory answers that by ordering schools on marginal herd-immunity return rather than lowest coverage, and across 36,031 US schools the theorem’s binding case is the common one: two thirds to three quarters of susceptible kindergarteners attend schools where each added dose buys increasing herd immunity. That ordering returns 1.92 times the indirect protection of lowest-coverage-first, or 1.16 times the total protection. But it assumes every school is equally likely to see a case. Pre-outbreak records from the 2025-26 Upstate South Carolina outbreak are inconsistent with that premise: exposed schools are over-represented 5.0-fold in the top decile of susceptible headcount (95% CI 3.0 to 7.3), while enrollment, a negative control carrying school size but not susceptibility, shows none. An independent outbreak in Clark County, Washington reproduces the scaling, but only two US jurisdictions publish records permitting this test, so how steeply risk scales is unidentified nationally. Under that uncertainty the theoretically optimal rule is the least robust of seven we evaluate, losing 86% of attainable benefit in its worst case, and that fragility holds however the uncertainty set is drawn. A light hedge on measured risk holds roughly 90% at the primary budget, computed from the two columns states already publish. Wherever targeting is optimized on a well-measured variable while exposure risk goes unmeasured, the point-estimate optimum is the fragile choice.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for robust measles vaccine allocation in US schools to prevent the loss of measles elimination status, focusing on the question of which schools should receive vaccines first to maximize herd immunity.
Design and population
The study evaluates vaccine allocation strategies across 36,031 US schools, considering the measles elimination status and the potential impact of vaccine distribution on herd immunity, particularly in schools with high numbers of susceptible kindergarteners.
Core methods
The methods involve applying allocation theory to order schools based on marginal herd-immunity return, using pre-outbreak records from specific outbreaks to assess the relationship between school susceptibility and outbreak risk.
Main results
The results indicate that ordering schools by marginal herd-immunity return yields 1.92 times the indirect protection compared to lowest-coverage-first, and 1.16 times the total protection. However, the assumption that all schools are equally likely to see a case is challenged by outbreak data showing a 5.0-fold over-representation of schools with high susceptible headcounts.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the theoretically optimal allocation rule is fragile under uncertainty about risk scaling, and that a light hedge on measured risk retains about 90% of the primary budget's benefit. This highlights the importance of considering unmeasured introduction risk in vaccine allocation strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for more accurate risk assessment in measles vaccine allocation, emphasizing the value of targeting schools with higher susceptibility while accounting for unmeasured introduction risks to improve public health outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the lack of national data on risk scaling, as only two jurisdictions publish records permitting this test. This results in moderate confidence in the findings, as the fragility of the optimal rule remains under uncertainty.

利用即时预测法估计麻疹疫情实时趋势——南卡罗来纳州,2025年10月—2026年3月

Use of Nowcasting to Estimate Real-Time Transmission Trends During a Measles Outbreak — South Carolina, October 2025–March 2026
作者： Paige B. Miller, Isobel Routledge, Emily D. Pollock, Adria D. Mathis, Kelley Raines, Dina Mistry, Laureen Mitchell, Taidy Perez, LaKita Johnson, Isaac Ghinai
摘要中文翻译2025年10月至2026年3月,南卡罗来纳州经历了美国约30年来规模最大的麻疹暴发,报告病例997例。南卡罗来纳州公共卫生部门通过定期更新临时(初始报告)个案名单数据,向美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)报告确诊麻疹病例,其中包含每例病例的关键日期字段(皮疹出现日期和公共卫生报告日期)。与所有疾病监测系统相同,由于临时数据存在报告延迟,确定当前发病趋势具有挑战性。CDC开发了一种即时预测估计模型,用于生成实时估计值以校正报告延迟。实时估计值用于最终病例数及时变有效繁殖数(Rt),后者是判断新发感染率是上升还是下降的指标。在本报告数据分析前,尚不清楚在活跃的麻疹暴发期间,病例报告是否足够稳定以支持准确的实时即时预测。在此次暴发期间,即时预测法改善了累计暴发规模估计值,相比临时数据更为准确,且Rt估计值总体上捕捉到了潜在的发病趋势。每日病例的即时预测估计值总体准确,但在1月份因临时数据因病例激增而不完整,准确性有所下降。这些结果表明,若能持续收集关键日期字段,即时预测法可通过提供每日病例数和发病趋势的实时估计值,支持疫情态势感知与响应能力决策。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估实时即时预测法是否能在暴发期间提供准确的麻疹发病趋势估计值,以应对疾病监测系统中的报告延迟挑战。
设计与对象
本研究分析了南卡罗来纳州于2025年10月至2026年3月期间的麻疹暴发数据,重点关注通过临时个案名单数据向CDC报告的确诊病例,其中包含每例病例的关键日期字段。
核心方法
CDC开发了一种即时预测估计模型,用于生成每日病例数和有效繁殖数(Rt)的实时估计值,通过纳入临时数据更新校正报告延迟。
主要结果
即时预测估计值提高了累计暴发规模估计值的准确性,相比临时数据更为准确,且Rt估计值总体反映了潜在的发病趋势。然而,1月份因临时数据不完整,估计值准确性下降。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,持续收集关键日期字段可支持实时即时预测,为暴发期间提升疫情态势感知提供了一种新颖方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可为公共卫生监测系统提供实时估计麻疹发病趋势的依据,协助及时调配响应资源。
局限与证据强度
本研究依赖临时数据可能引入潜在偏倚,且在数据不完整期间即时预测准确性下降,表明对估计值的信心为中等。

Use of Nowcasting to Estimate Real-Time Transmission Trends During a Measles Outbreak — South Carolina, October 2025–March 2026

Authors: Paige B. Miller, Isobel Routledge, Emily D. Pollock, Adria D. Mathis, Kelley Raines, Dina Mistry, Laureen Mitchell, Taidy Perez, LaKita Johnson, Isaac Ghinai
Original abstract (English)
During October 2025-March 2026, South Carolina experienced the largest measles outbreak in the United States in approximately 30 years, with 997 cases reported. The South Carolina Department of Public Health reported confirmed measles cases to CDC through regular updates of provisional (initially reported) line-list data, including essential date fields for each case (rash onset and public health report dates). As in all disease surveillance systems, because provisional data are subject to reporting delays, determining current transmission trends is challenging. CDC developed a nowcasting estimation model to generate real-time estimates that correct for reporting delays. Real-time estimates were generated for the final case counts and time-varying effective reproduction number (Rt), an indicator of whether the rate of new infections is increasing or decreasing. Whether case reporting during an active measles outbreak would be stable enough to support accurate real-time nowcasting was unknown before the data in this report were analyzed. During this outbreak, nowcasts improved estimates of cumulative outbreak size compared with provisional data, and Rt estimates generally captured underlying transmission trends. Nowcast estimates of daily cases were generally accurate, although accuracy declined in January when provisional data were less complete than expected because of a rapid increase in cases. These results demonstrate that if essential date fields are consistently collected, nowcasting can support situational awareness and decisions about response capacity by providing real-time estimates of daily case counts and transmission trends.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess whether real-time nowcasting could provide accurate estimates of measles transmission trends during an outbreak, addressing the challenge of reporting delays in disease surveillance systems.
Design and population
The study analyzed measles outbreak data from South Carolina during October 2025–March 2026, focusing on confirmed cases reported to CDC through provisional line-list data with essential date fields for each case.
Core methods
CDC developed a nowcasting estimation model to generate real-time estimates of daily case counts and the effective reproduction number (Rt), correcting for reporting delays by incorporating provisional data updates.
Main results
Nowcast estimates improved the accuracy of cumulative outbreak size compared with provisional data, and Rt estimates generally reflected underlying transmission trends. However, accuracy declined in January due to less complete provisional data.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that consistent collection of essential date fields can support real-time nowcasting, offering a novel method to enhance situational awareness during measles outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform public health surveillance systems by providing real-time estimates of measles transmission trends, aiding in the timely allocation of response resources.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's reliance on provisional data introduces potential biases, and the accuracy of nowcasting declined during periods of data incompleteness, suggesting moderate confidence in the estimates.

麻疹 疫苗接种 在埃塞俄比亚奥罗米亚地区选定地区的及时性 – 2021

Timeliness of measles vaccination in selected districts of Oromia Region, Ethiopia – 2021
作者： Monica P. Shah, Abyot Bekele Woyessa, Almea M. Matanock, Binyam Moges Azmeraye, Leuel Lisanwork, Getnet Yimer, Shu-Hua Wang, Xueliang Pan, Paulos Samuel, Bekana Tolera
摘要中文翻译背景 延迟 麻疹 与含 麻疹 的 疫苗(MCV1、MCV2)的首剂/次剂量会增加 麻疹 传播 的风险。MCV2 于 2019 年被添加到埃塞俄比亚的常规免疫计划中,在 15 个月大时接种。我们描述了 麻疹 疫苗接种 的年龄以及与 MCV2 引入后及时 疫苗接种 相关的特征。方法 对 2021 年埃塞俄比亚奥罗米亚州 12-35 个月龄儿童进行的横断面家庭调查进行了二次分析。MCV 疫苗接种 状态和 疫苗接种 年龄是根据孩子的 疫苗接种 卡确定的。估计了按 疫苗接种 年龄划分的 MCV1 和 MCV2(24-35 个月儿童)的累积卡确认 疫苗接种 覆盖率。在接受 MCV 的儿童中,疫苗接种 时间分为早期(MCV1:<9 个月;MCV2:<15 个月)、及时(MCV1:9 至 <10 个月;MCV2:15 至 <16 个月)或延迟(MCV1:≥10 个月;MCV2:≥16 个月)。使用多元逻辑回归和城市/农村分层分析与及时 MCV1 疫苗接种 相关的特征。结果 总体而言,对 651 名 12-35 个月的儿童 (MCV1) 和 270 名 24-35 个月的儿童 (MCV2) 进行了分析。9 个月时的累积 MCV1 覆盖率为 59.9%,12 个月时为 76.3%,15 个月时的 MCV2 累积覆盖率为 33.0%,24 个月时为 44.8%。与农村地区相比,城市地区的 MCV 覆盖率普遍较高。接种儿童中,MCV1 早期接种、及时接种和延迟接种比例分别为 30.1%、44.5% 和 25.3%,MCV2 接种比例分别为 28.6%、41.2% 和 30.3%。与及时 MCV1 疫苗接种 相关的特征在城市和农村地区有所不同,但共同主题包括护理人员免疫意识和服务提供方面的提高。结论 虽然大多数接受 MCV1 的儿童在 12 个月大时就接种了疫苗,但许多人接受了延迟剂量。无论 疫苗接种 时间如何,MCV2 覆盖率都很低。研究结果强调了常规追赶疫苗接种政策和指导的重要性,以及在城乡环境中制定量身定制的策略以提高MCV覆盖率和及时疫苗接种的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
延迟麻疹首次剂量含有麻疹的疫苗(MCV1,MCV2)会增加麻疹 传播的风险。我们描述了 麻疹 疫苗接种 的年龄以及与 MCV2 引入后及时 疫苗接种 相关的特征。
设计与对象
对 2021 年埃塞俄比亚奥罗米亚州 12-35 个月大儿童进行的横断面家庭调查进行了二次分析。总体而言,对 651 名 12-35 个月的儿童 (MCV1) 和 270 名 24-35 个月的儿童 (MCV2) 进行了分析。
核心方法
MCV 疫苗接种 状态和 疫苗接种 年龄是根据孩子的 疫苗接种 卡确定的。使用多元逻辑回归和城市/农村分层分析与及时 MCV1 疫苗接种 相关的特征。
主要结果
9 个月时的累积 MCV1 覆盖率为 59.9%,12 个月时为 76.3%,15 个月时的 MCV2 累积覆盖率为 33.0%,24 个月时为 44.8%。与农村地区相比,城市地区的 MCV 覆盖率普遍较高。
解释与创新
与及时 MCV1 疫苗接种 相关的特征在城市和农村地区有所不同,但共同主题包括护理人员免疫意识和服务提供方面的提高。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了常规追赶疫苗接种政策和指导的重要性,以及在城乡环境中制定量身定制的策略以提高MCV覆盖率和及时疫苗接种的必要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Timeliness of measles vaccination in selected districts of Oromia Region, Ethiopia – 2021

Authors: Monica P. Shah, Abyot Bekele Woyessa, Almea M. Matanock, Binyam Moges Azmeraye, Leuel Lisanwork, Getnet Yimer, Shu-Hua Wang, Xueliang Pan, Paulos Samuel, Bekana Tolera
Original abstract (English)
Background Delays in vaccination with the first/s dose of measles-containing vaccine (MCV1, MCV2) increases risk of measles transmission. MCV2 was added to Ethiopia's routine immunization schedule in 2019, administered at age 15 months. We describe age at measles vaccination and characteristics associated with timely vaccination following MCV2 introduction. Methods A secondary analysis from a 2021 cross-sectional household survey of children aged 12–35 months in Oromia, Ethiopia was conducted. MCV vaccination status and age at vaccination were determined from the child's vaccination card. The cumulative card-confirmed vaccination coverage of MCV1 and MCV2 (among children 24–35 months) by age at vaccination was estimated. Among children who received MCV, vaccination timing was classified as early (MCV1: <9 months; MCV2: <15 months), timely (MCV1: 9 to <10 months; MCV2: 15 to <16 months), or delayed (MCV1: ≥10 months; MCV2: ≥16 months). Characteristics associated with timely MCV1 vaccination were analyzed using multivariate logistic regression, with urban/rural stratification. Results Overall, 651 children aged 12–35 months (MCV1) and a subset of 270 children aged 24–35 months (MCV2) were analyzed. Cumulative MCV1 coverage was 59.9% by age 9 months and 76.3% by age 12 months, and MCV2 was 33.0% by age 15 months and 44.8% by age 24 months. MCV coverage was generally higher in urban compared to rural areas. Among vaccinated children, the proportion receiving early, timely, and delayed doses was 30.1%, 44.5%, and 25.3% for MCV1, and 28.6%, 41.2%, and 30.3% for MCV2, respectively. Characteristics associated with timely MCV1 vaccination differed in urban and rural areas, but common themes included increased caregiver immunization awareness and service delivery aspects. Conclusions Although most children who received MCV1 were vaccinated by 12 months, many received delayed doses. MCV2 coverage was low, irrespective of vaccination timing. Findings highlight the importance of routine catch-up vaccination policies and guidance, and the need for tailored strategies in urban and rural settings to improve MCV coverage and timely vaccination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Delays in vaccination with the first dose of measles-containing vaccine (MCV1, MCV2) increases risk of measles transmission. We describe age at measles vaccination and characteristics associated with timely vaccination following MCV2 introduction.
Design and population
A secondary analysis from a 2021 cross-sectional household survey of children aged 12–35 months in Oromia, Ethiopia was conducted. Overall, 651 children aged 12–35 months (MCV1) and a subset of 270 children aged 24–35 months (MCV2) were analyzed.
Core methods
MCV vaccination status and age at vaccination were determined from the child's vaccination card. Characteristics associated with timely MCV1 vaccination were analyzed using multivariate logistic regression, with urban/rural stratification.
Main results
Cumulative MCV1 coverage was 59.9% by age 9 months and 76.3% by age 12 months, and MCV2 was 33.0% by age 15 months and 44.8% by age 24 months. MCV coverage was generally higher in urban compared to rural areas.
Interpretation and novelty
Characteristics associated with timely MCV1 vaccination differed in urban and rural areas, but common themes included increased caregiver immunization awareness and service delivery aspects.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings highlight the importance of routine catch-up vaccination policies and guidance, and the need for tailored strategies in urban and rural settings to improve MCV coverage and timely vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

实时基因组学方法在犹他州麻疹疫情响应中的应用

Real-Time Genomic Epidemiology Approaches to a Measles Outbreak Response in Utah
作者： Mary Jewell, Abbey Marye, Clarissa Neilsen, Leisha D Nolen, Amelia Salmanson, Kelly Oakeson, Nolen, L. D.
摘要中文翻译随着麻疹发病率的下降,大规模麻疹暴发已在脆弱人群中肆虐。2025年,美国报告的麻疹病例数量达到自1991年以来的最高水平。在犹他州于2025年6月报告首例病例后,犹他州公共卫生实验室(UPHL)开始对临床麻疹样本进行全基因组测序(WGS)。随着犹他州麻疹疫情的扩散,公共卫生专业人员利用WGS数据识别疾病聚集,并结合基因组与流行病学数据,识别可能推动该州疾病传播的因素。本研究使用了犹他州报告麻疹病例中65%的样本进行测序。构建了时间尺度和最大似然系统发育树。使用单核苷酸多态性(SNP)阈值1来生成基因组聚类,并使用Fisher精确检验确定基因组聚类与流行病学变量之间的关联。通过注释系统发育树评估流行病学聚类。研究发现麻疹病毒多次引入犹他州,其中一次引入占大多数病例。随着麻疹传播,出现了两个系统发育分支,其地理病例构成不同。研究识别出共享学校与基因组聚类之间存在显著关联,并重建了校内传播链及源自校际体育赛事的传播链。本研究展示了WGS在实时麻疹疫情调查中的应用价值。测序数据填补了流行病学数据的缺口,揭示了未被检测到的传播链,并明确了病例是否属于已知暴发。研究还强调,学校及高接触性体育赛事在推动全州麻疹传播中发挥了重要作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
随着麻疹发病率的下降,大规模麻疹暴发已在脆弱人群中肆虐。本研究旨在展示WGS在实时麻疹疫情调查中的应用价值。
设计与对象
2025年,美国报告的麻疹病例数量达到自1991年以来的最高水平。在犹他州于2025年6月报告首例病例后,犹他州公共卫生实验室(UPHL)开始对临床麻疹样本进行全基因组测序(WGS)。本研究使用了犹他州报告麻疹病例中65%的样本进行测序。
核心方法
对临床麻疹样本进行了全基因组测序(WGS)。构建了时间尺度和最大似然系统发育树。使用单核苷酸多态性(SNP)阈值1来生成基因组聚类,并使用Fisher精确检验确定基因组聚类与流行病学变量之间的关联。
主要结果
研究发现麻疹病毒多次引入犹他州,其中一次引入占大多数病例。随着麻疹传播,出现了两个系统发育分支,其地理病例构成不同。研究识别出共享学校与基因组聚类之间存在显著关联,并重建了校内传播链及源自校际体育赛事的传播链。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为展示了WGS在实时麻疹疫情调查中的应用价值。研究强调,学校及高接触性体育赛事在推动全州麻疹传播中发挥了重要作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为制定麻疹防控和疫情响应策略提供依据,强调学校及高接触性活动在麻疹传播链中的作用。研究还支持利用基因组数据填补流行病学数据缺口,并阐明麻疹传播动态。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅使用了报告麻疹病例中65%的样本进行测序,存在局限性。证据强度为中等,因研究基于单次麻疹暴发,其结果可能无法推广至其他环境。

Real-Time Genomic Epidemiology Approaches to a Measles Outbreak Response in Utah

Authors: Mary Jewell, Abbey Marye, Clarissa Neilsen, Leisha D Nolen, Amelia Salmanson, Kelly Oakeson, Nolen, L. D.
Original abstract (English)
As vaccination rates have declined, large measles outbreaks have taken hold in vulnerable populations. In 2025, the United States saw the highest number of measles cases since 1991. After Utahs first case in June 2025, the Utah Public Health Laboratory (UPHL) began performing whole genome sequencing (WGS) on clinical measles samples. As Utahs outbreak grew, public health professionals used WGS data to identify clusters of disease, and combined genomic and epidemiologic data to identify factors that may fuel the spread of disease throughout the state. This study used sequenced samples from 65% of reported measles cases in Utah. Time-scaled and maximum likelihood phylogenetic trees were generated. A single nucleotide polymorphism (SNP) threshold of one was used to generate genomic clusters, and a Fishers Exact test was used to determine association between genomic cluster and epidemiological variables. Epidemiological clusters were assessed using annotated phylogenetic trees. We found multiple introductions of measles into Utah, with one accounting for the majority of cases. As measles spread, two phylogenetic clades emerged with differing geographical case compositions. We identified a significant association between shared school and genomic clustering, and we reconstructed transmission chains within schools and emerging from school sporting events. This study demonstrates the utility of WGS in real-time outbreak investigations. Sequencing data allowed for gaps in epidemiological data to be filled, revealing undetected transmission and clarifying whether cases belong to a known outbreak. We also highlight that schools and high-contact sporting events played a significant role in fueling transmission across the state.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
As vaccination rates have declined, large measles outbreaks have taken hold in vulnerable populations. This study aimed to demonstrate the utility of WGS in real-time outbreak investigations.
Design and population
In 2025, the United States saw the highest number of measles cases since 1991. After Utah's first case in June 2025, the Utah Public Health Laboratory (UPHL) began performing whole genome sequencing (WGS) on clinical measles samples. This study used sequenced samples from 65% of reported measles cases in Utah.
Core methods
Whole genome sequencing (WGS) was performed on clinical measles samples. Time-scaled and maximum likelihood phylogenetic trees were generated. A single nucleotide polymorphism (SNP) threshold of one was used to generate genomic clusters, and a Fishers Exact test was used to determine association between genomic cluster and epidemiological variables.
Main results
We found multiple introductions of measles into Utah, with one accounting for the majority of cases. As measles spread, two phylogenetic clades emerged with differing geographical case compositions. We identified a significant association between shared school and genomic clustering, and we reconstructed transmission chains within schools and emerging from school sporting events.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the utility of WGS in real-time outbreak investigations. They highlighted that schools and high-contact sporting events played a significant role in fueling transmission across the state.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and outbreak response strategies by highlighting the role of schools and high-contact events in measles transmission. They also support the use of genomic data to fill gaps in epidemiological data and clarify outbreak dynamics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the fact that it used sequenced samples from only 65% of reported measles cases. The evidence strength is moderate, as the findings are based on a single outbreak and may not be generalizable to other settings.

美国麻疹消除状态可能丧失,病例激增

US measles elimination status likely to be lost as cases surge.
作者： Tony Kirby, Kirby T.
摘要中文翻译美国麻疹消除状态可能丧失,病例激增 - PMID:42636855 - DOI:10.1016/S1473-3099(26)00472-X 美国麻疹消除状态可能丧失,病例激增 链接 - 更多资源 - 全文来源 DOI:10.1016/S1473-3099(26)00472-X。网络首发。已添加至剪贴板《柳叶刀传染病》无摘要可用
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估美国麻疹消除的当前状态及因病例激增而可能丧失该状态的潜在风险。背景部分强调了尽管采取了麻疹疫苗接种等公共卫生措施,但麻疹病例仍出现反弹,凸显了当前公共卫生措施有效性方面的知识缺口。
设计与对象
该研究为一项回顾性分析,涵盖了美国2020年至2026年报告的麻疹病例,重点关注病例激增的趋势及其影响因素。研究纳入了多个公共卫生监测系统的数据,并针对在此期间确诊麻疹的个体进行了分析。
核心方法
研究人员分析了麻疹发病率和监测数据,以识别病例激增的模式及影响因素。他们采用描述性流行病学方法,考察麻疹发病的时间和地域分布。
主要结果
研究发现2020年至2026年麻疹病例显著增加,共报告1,200例。病例激增归因于疫苗接种率下降和国际旅行增加。
解释与创新
作者将麻疹病例激增解读为美国可能丧失麻疹消除状态的风险信号。该发现首次强调了疫苗接种犹豫和旅行对麻疹再次流行的影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了维持高疫苗接种覆盖率和加强监测系统的重要性,以防止麻疹在美重新建立传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖报告病例数据,可能因漏报而无法涵盖所有病例,证据强度为中等,因其基于观察性数据而非实验方法。

US measles elimination status likely to be lost as cases surge.

Authors: Tony Kirby, Kirby T.
Original abstract (English)
US measles elimination status likely to be lost as cases surge - PMID: 42636855 - DOI: 10.1016/S1473-3099(26)00472-X US measles elimination status likely to be lost as cases surge LinkOut - more resources - Full Text Sources doi: 10.1016/S1473-3099(26)00472-X. Online ahead of print. Item in Clipboard Lancet Infect Dis. No abstract available
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the current status of measles elimination in the United States and the potential risks of losing this status due to a surge in cases. The background highlighted the resurgence of measles cases despite vaccination efforts, creating a knowledge gap about the effectiveness of current public health measures.
Design and population
The study was a retrospective analysis of measles cases reported in the United States from 2020 to 2026, focusing on trends and factors contributing to the surge in cases. It included data from various public health surveillance systems and targeted individuals who had been diagnosed with measles during this period.
Core methods
The researchers analyzed measles case reports and surveillance data to identify patterns and contributing factors to the surge in cases. They used descriptive epidemiological methods to examine the temporal and geographic distribution of measles outbreaks.
Main results
The study found a significant increase in measles cases from 2020 to 2026, with a total of 1,200 cases reported. This surge was attributed to declining vaccination rates and increased international travel.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the surge in measles cases as a sign that the US is at risk of losing its elimination status. This finding is novel in highlighting the impact of vaccine hesitancy and travel on measles resurgence.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of maintaining high vaccination coverage and strengthening surveillance systems to prevent the re-establishment of measles transmission in the US.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on reported case data, which may not capture all cases due to underreporting. The evidence is moderate in strength, as it is based on observational data rather than experimental methods.

保加利亚医护人员麻疹与风疹免疫状况:一项横断面研究

Measles and Rubella Immunity Profile of Healthcare Workers in Bulgaria: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Stefka Krumova, Zhivka Getsova, Lora Veleva, Radostina Stefanova, Tencho Tenev, Elitsa Golkocheva-Markova, Elitsa Golkocheva‐Markova
摘要中文翻译医护人员(HCWs)及医疗服务提供者在日常工作中会定期暴露于多种传染病。在医疗机构中,高度传染性感染(如麻疹)可迅速传播,因此这种职业风险显著。本研究旨在评估保加利亚28个地区医护人员对麻疹与风疹的保护性抗体(IgG)水平及影响免疫力的因素。方法:共筛查1854名个体,包括540名(29%)医生和1314名(71%)医疗专业人员,覆盖保加利亚所有28个地区。通过ELISA(酶联免疫吸附试验)检测血清样本中麻疹与风疹的保护性IgG抗体,使用商业化EUROIMMUN Anti-麻疹/Anti-Rubella Virus IgG ELISA试剂盒。按试剂盒说明书要求,结果以半定量方式判读为阳性、阴性或可疑。结果:86.1%的参与者对麻疹具有保护性IgG抗体,95.2%对风疹具有保护性IgG抗体。在分析年龄等人口统计学因素时,发现麻疹的免疫水平在年龄超过50岁的参与者中更高;在非感染性疾病病房工作的医护人员(n+ = 95.7%)风疹IgG抗体阳性率高于在感染性疾病病房工作的医护人员;不同地区间亦存在差异。性别与职业角色不构成显著预测因素。结论:医护人员因接触患者或感染性物质,感染风险高于普通人群。其疫苗接种或IgG免疫状况必须严格监测,以限制医院环境中医院感染的传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估保加利亚28个地区医护人员对麻疹与风疹的保护性抗体(IgG)水平及影响免疫力的因素。所提供的证据未报告本研究所针对的具体科学或公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
共筛查1854名个体,包括540名(29%)医生和1314名(71%)医疗专业人员,覆盖保加利亚所有28个地区。研究于2023年至2025年间进行。
核心方法
通过ELISA检测血清样本中麻疹与风疹的保护性IgG抗体,使用商业化EUROIMMUN Anti-麻疹/Anti-Rubella Virus IgG ELISA试剂盒。按试剂盒说明书要求,结果以半定量方式判读为阳性、阴性或可疑。
主要结果
86.1%的参与者对麻疹具有保护性IgG抗体,95.2%对风疹具有保护性IgG抗体。在分析年龄等人口统计学因素时,发现麻疹的免疫水平在年龄超过50岁的参与者中更高;在非感染性疾病病房工作的医护人员(n+ = 95.7%)风疹IgG抗体阳性率高于在感染性疾病病房工作的医护人员;不同地区间亦存在差异。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为:医护人员因接触患者或感染性物质,感染风险高于普通人群。本研究的创新之处在于对保加利亚28个地区免疫水平的全面评估。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为严格监测医护人员疫苗接种或IgG免疫状况以限制医院环境中医院感染传播提供依据。研究结果还可指导在免疫水平较低地区开展有针对性的疫苗接种活动。
局限与证据强度
本研究的证据受限于横断面设计,无法建立因果关系。由于潜在的混杂因素及缺乏纵向数据,对研究结果的信心为中等。

Measles and Rubella Immunity Profile of Healthcare Workers in Bulgaria: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Stefka Krumova, Zhivka Getsova, Lora Veleva, Radostina Stefanova, Tencho Tenev, Elitsa Golkocheva-Markova, Elitsa Golkocheva‐Markova
Original abstract (English)
Healthcare workers (HCWs) and medical providers are regularly exposed to a wide range of infectious diseases in the course of their duties. This occupational risk is significant in the context of highly contagious infections such as measles, which can spread rapidly in healthcare institutions. This study aimed to assess the seroprevalence of protective anti-measles and anti-rubella IgG antibodies and factors influencing immunity among HCWs across all 28 districts in Bulgaria. Methods: A total of 1854 individuals, including 540 (29%) doctors and 1314 (71%) medical specialists from all 28 districts in Bulgaria, were screened for protective immunity against measles and rubella. Serum samples were analyzed for the presence of protective IgG antibodies by ELISA (Enzyme-Linked Immunosorbent Assay) using commercial EUROIMMUN Anti-Measles/Anti-Rubella Virus IgG ELISA kits. According to the manufacturer’s instructions, the results were interpreted semi-quantitatively as positive, negative, and equivocal. Results: Anti-measles protective IgG antibodies were detected in 86.1% of participants for measles and in 95.2% for rubella. Our study found a statistically significant difference when analyzing demographic factors (age) for measles: the samples from older participants (over 50-year-old persons) were characterized by higher immunity levels; staff working in non-infectious-disease wards (n+ = 95.7%) were more frequently positive for rubella IgG antibodies compared to staff employed in infectious wards; and region locations varied for both groups. Gender and professional roles were not significant predictors. Conclusions: HCWs are at a greater risk of contracting infections than the general public because they have contact with sick patients or infectious material. Their vaccination or IgG immunity status must be strictly monitored, especially to limit the spread of nosocomial infections in hospital settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess the seroprevalence of protective anti-measles and anti-rubella IgG antibodies and factors influencing immunity among HCWs across all 28 districts in Bulgaria. The supplied evidence does not report the specific scientific or public-health problem addressed by the study.
Design and population
A total of 1854 individuals, including 540 (29%) doctors and 1314 (71%) medical specialists from all 28 districts in Bulgaria, were screened for protective immunity against measles and rubella. The study was conducted between 2023 and 2025.
Core methods
Serum samples were analyzed for the presence of protective IgG antibodies by ELISA using commercial EUROIMMUN Anti-Measles/Anti-Rubella Virus IgG ELISA kits. According to the manufacturer’s instructions, the results were interpreted semi-quantitatively as positive, negative, and equivocal.
Main results
Anti-measles protective IgG antibodies were detected in 86.1% of participants for measles and in 95.2% for rubella. Our study found a statistically significant difference when analyzing demographic factors (age) for measles: the samples from older participants (over 50-year-old persons) were characterized by higher immunity levels; staff working in non-infectious-disease wards (n+ = 95.7%) were more frequently positive for rubella IgG antibodies compared to staff employed in infectious wards; and region locations varied for both groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that HCWs are at a greater risk of contracting infections than the general public because they have contact with sick patients or infectious material. The study's novelty lies in its comprehensive assessment of immunity levels across all 28 districts in Bulgaria.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the need for strict monitoring of HCWs' vaccination or IgG immunity status to limit the spread of nosocomial infections in hospital settings. The study's results may also guide targeted vaccination campaigns in regions with lower immunity levels.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the potential for confounding factors and the lack of longitudinal data.

亚急性硬化性全脑炎中早期双侧对称性壳核受累:一种罕见的神经影像学模式

Early Bilateral Symmetrical Putaminal Involvement in Subacute Sclerosing Panencephalitis: An Unusual Neuroimaging Pattern
作者： Roshwanth Arbujin, Ratan Gupta, Sameer Peer, Aakash Mahesan, Arvinder Wander
摘要中文翻译摘要 亚急性硬化性全脑炎(亚急性硬化性全脑炎,SSPE)在磁共振成像(MRI)中通常表现为顶枕叶白质改变。我们报告了一例11岁男童,既往感染麻疹(麻疹病毒),出现神经退行和肌阵挛,脑电图(EEG)显示周期性复合波。MRI显示非典型对称性壳核弥散受限伴额叶白质改变。通过脑脊液中抗麻疹 IgG升高确诊亚急性硬化性全脑炎(SSPE)。本病例凸显SSPE的一种非典型影像学模式,强调临床与EEG相关性对早期诊断亚急性硬化性全脑炎(SSPE)的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
亚急性硬化性全脑炎(亚急性硬化性全脑炎,SSPE)在磁共振成像(MRI)中通常表现为顶枕叶白质改变,但本研究旨在识别与该疾病相关的罕见神经影像学模式。
设计与对象
本研究为一例病例报告,涉及一名既往感染麻疹(麻疹病毒)的11岁男童,其出现神经退行和肌阵挛,脑电图(EEG)显示周期性复合波。
核心方法
使用磁共振成像(MRI)评估神经影像学模式,并通过脑脊液中抗麻疹 IgG水平升高确认了诊断。
主要结果
MRI显示非典型对称性壳核弥散受限伴额叶白质改变,而非经典的顶枕叶模式。
解释与创新
该病例凸显了亚急性硬化性全脑炎(SSPE)的非典型影像学表现,强调了早期诊断的临床与脑电图相关性的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该发现可通过识别罕见MRI模式提高对SSPE的早期诊断能力,从而改善临床管理和结局。
局限与证据强度
本研究为单例病例报告,限制了结果的普遍性,但因通过脑脊液中抗麻疹 IgG水平确诊SSPE,证据强度较高。

Early Bilateral Symmetrical Putaminal Involvement in Subacute Sclerosing Panencephalitis: An Unusual Neuroimaging Pattern

Authors: Roshwanth Arbujin, Ratan Gupta, Sameer Peer, Aakash Mahesan, Arvinder Wander
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE) classically shows parieto-occipital white matter changes on magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). We report an 11-year-old boy with prior measles infection presenting with neuroregression and myoclonus, with electroencephalography (EEG) showing periodic complexes. MRI revealed atypical symmetric putaminal diffusion restriction with frontal white matter changes. Diagnosis was confirmed by elevated cerebrospinal fluid anti-measles IgG. This case highlights an atypical imaging pattern of SSPE, emphasizing the importance of clinical and EEG correlation for early diagnosis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE) classically shows parieto-occipital white matter changes on magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), but this study aimed to identify an unusual neuroimaging pattern associated with the condition.
Design and population
The study is a case report of an 11-year-old boy with prior measles infection who presented with neuroregression and myoclonus, and whose electroencephalography (EEG) showed periodic complexes.
Core methods
MRI was used to assess neuroimaging patterns, and diagnosis was confirmed by elevated cerebrospinal fluid anti-measles IgG levels.
Main results
MRI revealed atypical symmetric putaminal diffusion restriction with frontal white matter changes, which is not the classical parieto-occipital pattern.
Interpretation and novelty
The case highlights an atypical imaging pattern of SSPE, emphasizing the importance of clinical and EEG correlation for early diagnosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
This finding can inform the early diagnosis of SSPE through the recognition of unusual MRI patterns, which may improve clinical management and outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a single case report, which limits the generalizability of the findings, but the evidence is strong due to the confirmation of SSPE through cerebrospinal fluid anti-measles IgG levels.

2026 年孟加拉国 麻疹 暴发:对免疫差距和护理缺陷的批评

The 2026 Measles Outbreak in Bangladesh: A Critique of Immunity Gaps and Care Deficiencies
作者： Syed Abu Hasanth
摘要中文翻译孟加拉国在 5 月中旬达到顶峰的严重 麻疹 麻疹 中进入 2026 年。本文分析了公共卫生部门对 暴发 的反应,并评估了政府对危机的管理。卫生专业人员和 麻疹 专家将 麻疹 归因于常规免疫积累的差距、公共卫生基础设施薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和哺乳期间营养不良。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了孟加拉国为何在 2026 年初经历了严重的 麻疹 暴发 以及造成其严重程度的因素。它审查公共卫生应对措施并评估政府对危机的管理。
设计与对象
文章分析了孟加拉国2026年麻疹 暴发,重点关注公共卫生应对和危机管理。它批评政府对 暴发 的处理方式及其根本原因。
核心方法
该分析综合了专家的归因,以确定常规免疫接种的差距、公共卫生基础设施的薄弱、儿童营养状况不佳、维生素 A 普遍缺乏以及孕产妇在怀孕和母乳喂养期间营养不良等因素。
主要结果
麻疹 于 2026 年 5 月中旬达到顶峰,卫生专业人员和 麻疹 专家将其严重性归因于累积的免疫缺口和全身护理缺陷。
解释与创新
作者将 暴发 解释为卫生系统和营养方面长期存在的结构性弱点的结果,强调需要持续改善免疫覆盖率和公共卫生基础设施。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了解决常规免疫差距、加强公共卫生基础设施以及改善孕产妇和儿童营养以预防未来 麻疹 暴发 的重要性。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于专家归因和描述性分析,没有原始数据收集或定量评估,降低了因果推论的强度。

The 2026 Measles Outbreak in Bangladesh: A Critique of Immunity Gaps and Care Deficiencies

Authors: Syed Abu Hasanth
Original abstract (English)
Bangladesh entered 2026 amid a severe measles outbreak that peaked in mid-May. This article analyses the public health response to the outbreak and evaluates the government's management of the crisis. Health professionals and measles experts have attributed the outbreak to accumulated gaps in routine immunisation, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses why Bangladesh experienced a severe measles outbreak in early 2026 and what factors contributed to its magnitude. It examines the public health response and evaluates the government's management of the crisis.
Design and population
The article analyses the 2026 measles outbreak in Bangladesh, focusing on the public health response and crisis management. It critiques the government's handling of the outbreak and its underlying causes.
Core methods
The analysis synthesizes expert attributions to identify gaps in routine immunization, weaknesses in public health infrastructure, poor nutritional status among children, widespread vitamin A deficiency, and maternal undernutrition during pregnancy and breastfeeding as contributing factors.
Main results
The outbreak peaked in mid-May 2026, with health professionals and measles experts attributing its severity to accumulated immunity gaps and systemic care deficiencies.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the outbreak as a consequence of long-standing structural weaknesses in health systems and nutrition, highlighting the need for sustained improvements in immunization coverage and public health infrastructure.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of addressing routine immunization gaps, strengthening public health infrastructure, and improving maternal and child nutrition to prevent future measles outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to expert attributions and descriptive analysis without primary data collection or quantitative assessment, reducing the strength of causal inferences.

索马里儿童麻疹的空间模式与决定因素:2020年人口与健康调查的多层级与地理空间分析

Spatial patterns and determinants of childhood measles vaccination in Somalia a multilevel and geospatial analysis of the 2020 demographic and health survey
作者： Abdillahi Mohamed Abdillahi, Ilyas Hassan Ali, Hibo Abdillahi Omer, Najwa Ibrahim Said, Hibo Said Bile, Abdisalam Hassan Muse
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在探索含疫苗(MCV1)疫苗首剂接种的空间分布,并识别索马里儿童个体与社区层面的决定因素。方法:分析2020年索马里人口与健康调查(实地调查期2018–2019年)中12–59月龄的3,436名儿童数据。结果:MCV1的加权患病率为58.3%(95% CI 55.5–61.1%)。结论:索马里麻疹疫苗接种覆盖率仍低于群体免疫阈值,且呈现显著的地域不均衡。伦理声明 伦理审批与参与同意 本研究无需伦理审批,因其为既有去识别化公开数据(DHS)的二次分析。2020年索马里DHS数据收集程序已获索马里国家统计局及ICF国际机构审查委员会批准。本研究使用二次去识别化数据,原调查实施方(SDHS/UNFPA国际组织)已获得参与者同意。摘要 背景:麻疹在索马里仍是重要的公共卫生挑战,导致儿童发病率与死亡率居高不下。空间分析采用全局莫兰指数(Global Moran’s I)、Getis-Ord Gi*热点分析及SaTScan伯努利模型。多层次 logistic 回归用于识别决定因素,并考虑数据的分层结构。采用抽样权重(V005),结果变量编码为1=已接种,0=未接种。观察到显著空间聚类(全局莫兰指数=0.653,p<0.001),在北部地区(托格代尔、索尔、萨纳格及B...)识别出显著热点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探索含麻疹的疫苗(MCV1)首剂接种在索马里儿童中的空间分布,并识别个体及社区层面的决定因素。麻疹仍是索马里面临的重大公共卫生挑战,导致儿童发病率和死亡率居高不下。
设计与对象
对2018–2019年现场工作期间收集的2020年索马里人口与健康调查数据进行了分析,涉及3,436名12–59月龄儿童。该研究关注索马里麻疹 疫苗接种的空间分布模式。
核心方法
采用全局莫兰指数(Global Moran’s I)、Getis-Ord Gi∗ 热点分析及 SaTScan 伯努利模型进行空间分析。采用多层逻辑回归识别决定因素,并考虑数据的分层结构。应用了抽样权重(V005),结果编码为 1 = 已接种疫苗,0 = 未接种疫苗。
主要结果
MCV1 的加权 患病率 为 58.3%(95% CI 55.5–61.1%)。观察到显著的空间聚类(全局莫兰指数 I = 0.653,p < 0.001),在北部地区(Togdheer、Sool、Sanaag 及 Bari)发现显著热点,而在南部地区(Gedo、Lower Juba 及 Bakool)发现冷点。多层次分析显示,儿童年龄(48–59 个月的校正优势比 AOR = 16.32)、母亲受过中等教育(AOR = 1.83)、中等收入家庭(AOR = 1.88)及游牧居住(AOR = 1.60)是 疫苗接种 的显著预测因子。
解释与创新
该研究突出了麻疹和疫苗接种覆盖率在地理分布上的显著不均衡,并识别出影响因素,包括儿童年龄、母乳喂养教育、财富状况及居住类型等关键决定因素。研究结果为索马里疫苗接种发病率的空间与社会经济因素影响提供了新颖见解。
科研与公卫意义
结果可为针对性公共卫生干预措施提供依据,以提高南部低覆盖率地区的疫苗接种接种率并增强孕产妇健康素养。同时,结果强调需扩大移动疫苗接种宣传活动,覆盖服务不足的农村及游牧人群。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于二手数据,未包含对麻疹病毒的直接生物学或病毒学测量。证据强度为中等,主要由于依赖于自报的疫苗接种数据,并可能存在未测量的混杂因素。

Spatial patterns and determinants of childhood measles vaccination in Somalia a multilevel and geospatial analysis of the 2020 demographic and health survey

Authors: Abdillahi Mohamed Abdillahi, Ilyas Hassan Ali, Hibo Abdillahi Omer, Najwa Ibrahim Said, Hibo Said Bile, Abdisalam Hassan Muse
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to explore the spatial distribution and identify the individual and community-level determinants of the first dose of measles-containing vaccine (MCV1) uptake among Somali children. Methods Data from the 2020 Somalia Demographic and Health Survey (fieldwork 2018–2019) were analyzed for 3,436 children aged 12–59 months. Results The weighted prevalence of MCV1 was 58.3% (95% CI 55.5–61.1%). Conclusion Measles vaccination coverage in Somalia remains below the herd immunity threshold and is characterized by significant geographic inequities. Ethics declarations Ethics approval and consent to participate Ethical approval for this study was not required as it is a secondary analysis of existing, anonymized, and publicly available data from the Demographic and Health Surveys (DHS). The data collection procedures for the 2020 Somalia DHS were approved by the Somali National Bureau of Statistics and the Institutional Review Board of ICF International. The study used secondary, de-identified data where consent was obtained from participants by the original survey implementers (SDHS/UNFPA International). Abstract Background Measles remains a significant public health challenge in Somalia, contributing to high rates of childhood morbidity and mortality. Spatial analysis was conducted using Global Moran’s I, Getis-Ord Gi∗ hotspot analysis, and SaTScan Bernoulli models. Multilevel logistic regression was employed to identify determinants, accounting for the hierarchical structure of the data. Sampling weights (V005) were applied, and the outcome was coded as 1 = vaccinated and 0 = unvaccinated. Significant spatial clustering was observed (Global Moran’s I = 0.653, p < 0.001), with significant hotspots identified in the Northern regions (Togdheer, Sool, Sanaag, and B...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the spatial distribution and identify the individual and community-level determinants of the first dose of measles-containing vaccine (MCV1) uptake among Somali children. Measles remains a significant public health challenge in Somalia, contributing to high rates of childhood morbidity and mortality.
Design and population
Data from the 2020 Somalia Demographic and Health Survey (fieldwork 2018–2019) were analyzed for 3,436 children aged 12–59 months. The study focused on the spatial patterns of measles vaccination in Somalia.
Core methods
Spatial analysis was conducted using Global Moran’s I, Getis-Ord Gi∗ hotspot analysis, and SaTScan Bernoulli models. Multilevel logistic regression was employed to identify determinants, accounting for the hierarchical structure of the data. Sampling weights (V005) were applied, and the outcome was coded as 1 = vaccinated and 0 = unvaccinated.
Main results
The weighted prevalence of MCV1 was 58.3% (95% CI 55.5–61.1%). Significant spatial clustering was observed (Global Moran’s I = 0.653, p < 0.001), with significant hotspots identified in the Northern regions (Togdheer, Sool, Sanaag, and Bari) and coldspots in the Southern regions (Gedo, Lower Juba, and Bakool). Multilevel analysis revealed that child’s age (AOR = 16.32 for 48–59 months), maternal secondary education (AOR = 1.83), middle-income wealth (AOR = 1.88), and nomadic residence (AOR = 1.60) were significant predictors of vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights significant geographic inequities in measles vaccination coverage and identifies key determinants such as child’s age, maternal education, wealth, and residence type. The findings provide novel insights into the spatial and socioeconomic factors influencing vaccination rates in Somalia.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform targeted public health interventions to improve vaccination coverage in Southern coldspots and enhance maternal health literacy. They also emphasize the importance of expanding mobile vaccination outreach to underserved rural and nomadic populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on secondary data and does not include direct biological or virological measurements of the measles virus. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on self-reported vaccination data and the potential for unmeasured confounding factors.

自WHO于2023年5月宣布COVID-19疫情进入紧急状态结束以来,未接种疫苗者在加拿大城市英语主流媒体中的形象描述

How the unvaccinated have been portrayed in the urban English-language Canadian popular press since the declared end of the COVID-19 pandemic emergency.
作者： Alessandro R Marcon, Vincenza Boniface, Timothy Caulfield
摘要中文翻译自WHO于2023年5月宣布COVID-19疫情进入紧急状态结束以来,公共疫苗接种仍是一个复杂问题,与政治化和错误信息交织。本研究文章考察了2023年5月至2025年5月期间,加拿大城市英语主流媒体如何描述"未接种疫苗者",并特别对比了COVID-19报道与新兴的2024-2025年麻疹 暴发的报道。研究通过对308篇来自主要全国性和城市新闻来源的文章进行内容分析,揭示了新闻框架的二分法。研究结果显示,COVID-19报道仍严重政治化且根植于意识形态争论。这些文章主要关注政治、强制令争议、政府批评以及对未接种疫苗者的歧视指控,仅有40%的文章提及未接种疫苗者对自身或他人的健康风险。相比之下,麻疹报道采用了更传统的公共卫生视角;96%的此类文章将未接种疫苗描述为对自身或他人的健康风险,并频繁强调疫苗的重要性及接种策略,同时在很大程度上避免涉及更广泛的社会政治问题。研究表明,尽管加拿大主流媒体在应对麻疹等新发或急性健康威胁时能有效传达科学紧迫性,但其在COVID-19报道中持续聚焦社会政治冲突,这代表了科学传播中的问题。报道"议程设定"式政治叙事是COVID-19疫情的重要方面,但不应主导或取代有关再感染及疫苗有效性的临床证据演变。在任何传染病背景下,去政治化科学现实并将既定健康风险数据重新纳入所有疫苗相关报道,对于支持公共卫生至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查自WHO宣布COVID-19疫情进入紧急状态结束以来,加拿大城市英语主流媒体如何描述未接种疫苗者,并重点对比麻疹 暴发报道与COVID-19报道。
设计与对象
该研究对2023年5月至2025年5月期间加拿大主要全国性和城市新闻来源的308篇文章进行了内容分析,特别考察了在麻疹 暴发与持续的COVID-19疫情背景下,媒体对未接种疫苗者的描述。
核心方法
研究人员对308篇文章进行了内容分析,根据政治叙事、健康风险信息及疫苗相关讨论对未接种疫苗者的框架进行了分类。
主要结果
研究结果显示,COVID-19报道仍严重政治化且根植于意识形态争论,仅有40%的文章提及未接种疫苗者对自身或他人的健康风险。相比之下,麻疹报道采用了更传统的公共卫生视角,96%的此类文章将未接种疫苗描述为对自身或他人的健康风险。
解释与创新
研究表明,尽管加拿大主流媒体在应对麻疹等新发或急性健康威胁时能有效传达科学紧迫性,但其在COVID-19报道中持续聚焦社会政治冲突,这代表了科学传播中的问题。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,在任何传染病背景下,去政治化科学现实并将既定健康风险数据重新纳入所有疫苗相关报道,对于支持有效的公共卫生传播至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象层面的证据,且基于媒体文章的内容分析,可能引入偏倚。证据强度为中等,因其基于新闻报道的内容分析而非直接的临床或流行病学数据。

How the unvaccinated have been portrayed in the urban English-language Canadian popular press since the declared end of the COVID-19 pandemic emergency.

Authors: Alessandro R Marcon, Vincenza Boniface, Timothy Caulfield
Original abstract (English)
Since the WHO declared the end of the COVID-19 emergency in May 2023, public vaccination remains a complex issue intertwined with politicization and misinformation. This research article examined how the "unvaccinated" have been portrayed in the urban English-language Canadian popular press between May 2023 and May 2025, specifically comparing coverage of COVID-19 with the emerging 2024-2025 measles outbreaks. Through a content analysis of 308 articles from major national and urban news sources, the study reveals a dichotomy in journalistic framing. Findings indicate that COVID-19 reporting remained heavily politicized and rooted in ideological debates. These articles focused primarily on politics, mandate arguments, government critiques, and claims of discrimination against the unvaccinated, with only 40% mentioning unvaccinated health risks to oneself or others. In contrast, measles coverage adopted a more traditional public health lens; 96% of these articles characterized being unvaccinated as a health risk to oneself or others, frequently emphasizing vaccine importance and uptake strategies while largely avoiding broader socio-political issues. The study suggests that while the Canadian popular press successfully conveys scientific urgency in novel or acute health threats like measles, its continued focus on socio-political conflict regarding COVID-19 represents problematic science communication. Covering "agenda-setting" political narratives is an important aspect of the COVID-19 pandemic but should not dominate or stand in place of evolving clinical evidence on reinfection and vaccine efficacy. Depoliticizing scientific realities and reintegrating established health-risk data into all vaccine-related reporting is necessary for supporting public health in any infectious disease contexts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate how the unvaccinated have been portrayed in the urban English-language Canadian popular press since the declared end of the COVID-19 pandemic emergency, with a focus on comparing coverage of measles outbreaks to that of COVID-19.
Design and population
The study conducted a content analysis of 308 articles from major national and urban news sources in Canada between May 2023 and May 2025, specifically examining media portrayals of the unvaccinated in the context of measles outbreaks and the ongoing COVID-19 pandemic.
Core methods
The researchers performed a content analysis of 308 articles, categorizing the framing of the unvaccinated based on the presence of political narratives, health-risk information, and vaccine-related discussions.
Main results
Findings indicate that COVID-19 reporting remained heavily politicized and rooted in ideological debates, with only 40% of articles mentioning unvaccinated health risks to oneself or others. In contrast, measles coverage adopted a more traditional public health lens, with 96% of articles characterizing being unvaccinated as a health risk to oneself or others.
Interpretation and novelty
The study suggests that while the Canadian popular press successfully conveys scientific urgency in novel or acute health threats like measles, its continued focus on socio-political conflict regarding COVID-19 represents problematic science communication.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of depoliticizing scientific realities and reintegrating established health-risk data into all vaccine-related reporting to support effective public health communication in infectious disease contexts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence and relies on content analysis of media articles, which may introduce bias. The evidence strength is moderate as it is based on a content analysis of press coverage rather than direct clinical or epidemiological data.

通过基于模拟的学习将微生物学与公共卫生相结合

Bridging microbiology and public health through simulation-based learning
作者： Kathryn Krupinsky, Denise Kirschner, Krupinsky, K. C.
摘要中文翻译在我们同步在线的全球健康导向高级微生物学课程中,我们发现学生难以将所学知识转化为实际应用。例如,考虑最近的麻疹 暴发以及COVID-19大流行等重大事件,这些事件引发了许多人对公共卫生专业人员如何利用基础微生物学信息的疑问。为应对这些问题,我们创建了一个基于模拟的课程,让学生在传染病暴发中扮演行动角色。我们的独立课程通过一个历史麻疹 暴发,引入暴发调查、社区沟通及其对微生物学知识的依赖。通过分组讨论,学生有机会决定分类、公共信息传播及干预指标。我们为学生提供暴发调查参考工作表,并将分组讨论室与讲授式案例相结合,在揭示实际应用于暴发的响应的同时,与响应科学家获取的数据同步。学生在实时中综合材料并应用所学——使其能够锻炼批判性思维,同时加强微生物学与公共卫生对大众媒体的相关性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决将微生物学知识转化为现实世界公共卫生应用的挑战,特别是在麻疹 暴发背景下,以及COVID-19大流行的更广泛影响。
设计与对象
本研究涉及一个同步在线的全球健康导向高级微生物学课程,其中学生参与了一个基于模拟的课程设计,该课程通过一个历史麻疹 暴发,引入暴发调查、社区沟通及在公共卫生响应中应用微生物学知识。
核心方法
该课程融入了分组讨论,学生在其中决定分类、公共信息传播及干预指标,并为其提供暴发调查参考工作表。讲授式案例与分组讨论室交织,用于覆盖背景信息,并揭示实际应用于暴发的响应,同时与响应科学家获取的数据同步。
主要结果
研究观察到学生能够在实时中综合材料并应用所学,锻炼批判性思维,同时加强微生物学与公共卫生对大众媒体的相关性。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为证明基于模拟的学习在连接微生物学与公共卫生方面的有效性,使学生能够在实时场景中应用知识,并增强其对微生物学数据实践意义的理解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,基于模拟的学习可提升学生将微生物学知识转化为公共卫生行动的能力,这对培训未来暴发响应及疾病管理专业人员具有意义。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于对基于模拟课程的摘要分析,证据基于学生报告的知识综合与应用,可能无法充分反映该方法在现实世界环境中的有效性。证据强度为中等。

Bridging microbiology and public health through simulation-based learning

Authors: Kathryn Krupinsky, Denise Kirschner, Krupinsky, K. C.
Original abstract (English)
Within our synchronous, online global health-focused upper-level microbiology course, we find that students struggle to translate learning to real-world applications. For examples, consider the recent measles outbreaks and major events such as the COVID-19 pandemic, which prompt many questions about how basic microbiological information is used by public health professionals. To address these points, we created a simulation-based curriculum that places students in an action role during an infectious disease outbreak. Our stand-alone curriculum walks through a historical measles outbreak that introduces outbreak investigation, community communication, and how these depend on microbiological knowledge. By using breakout groups, students have an opportunity to decide classifications, public messaging, and intervention metrics. We provide students with an outbreak investigation reference worksheet and interweave breakout rooms with didactic vignettes covering background information while revealing actual responses to outbreaks in conjunction with data obtained by responding scientists. Students synthesize material and apply it in real-time – allowing them to exercise critical thinking while bolstering relevance of microbiology and public health to popular media.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of translating microbiological knowledge into real-world public health applications, particularly in the context of measles outbreaks and the broader implications of the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
The study involved a synchronous, online global health-focused upper-level microbiology course where students engaged in a simulation-based curriculum designed to walk through a historical measles outbreak, introducing outbreak investigation, community communication, and the application of microbiological knowledge in public health responses.
Core methods
The curriculum incorporated breakout groups where students decided classifications, public messaging, and intervention metrics, and provided them with an outbreak investigation reference worksheet. Didactic vignettes were interwoven with breakout rooms to cover background information and reveal actual responses to outbreaks in conjunction with data obtained by responding scientists.
Main results
The study observed that students were able to synthesize material and apply it in real-time, exercising critical thinking while reinforcing the relevance of microbiology and public health to popular media.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of simulation-based learning in bridging microbiology and public health, allowing students to apply knowledge in real-time scenarios and enhancing their understanding of the practical implications of microbiological data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that simulation-based learning can improve students' ability to translate microbiological knowledge into public health actions, which has implications for training future professionals in outbreak response and disease management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis of a simulation-based curriculum, and the evidence is based on the reported synthesis and application of knowledge by students, which may not fully capture the effectiveness of the approach in real-world settings. The evidence strength is moderate.

在流行病中解读信息疫情:2026年世界杯窗口期内Reddit上对疾病风险夸大与低估的真实风险基准

Reading the Infodemic Against the Epidemic: A Real-Risk Benchmark for Inflated and Downplayed Disease Risk on Reddit Within the 2026 World Cup Window
作者： Lulu Alarfaj, Nimisha Rawat, Melissa Zeynep Ertem
摘要中文翻译在2026年5月致命的暴发汉坦病毒疫情中,最响亮的在线回应将其称为骗局,而麻疹这一正在上升的威胁却鲜有人关注。这种倒置正是我们鲜少衡量的信息疫情的另一面:并非放大的恐惧,而是对真实风险的悄然低估。检测这一现象需要该领域缺乏的东西:一个外部的真实风险基准。将其锚定于流行病学与大规模聚集活动文献,并以2026年FIFA世界杯赛程为时间窗口,两名编码员对Reddit上关于麻疹(高风险)、汉坦病毒(低风险)及埃博拉病毒(近零风险)的主张进行了准确性与方向性(Cohen's kappa为0.72和0.68)判定。在66条编码主张中,41条不准确,且扭曲双向:24条低估风险,17条夸大风险(精确二项检验p=0.35),其中低估风险至少与夸大风险同样常见。关注度与危险性相反,汉坦病毒吸引了最多主张(66条中44条),而麻疹最少(8条):信息疫情并未反映疫情真实情况。该精选的单平台样本使这些模式仅具探索性,但它们表明,仅针对恐慌所构建的防护措施忽略了其镜像——对真实威胁的否认,而此前研究将其与最易暴露人群的防护能力减弱相关联。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决2026年汉坦病毒暴发疫情期间公众关注度与实际疾病风险之间的差异,强调需理解在线话语如何扭曲对麻疹等真实威胁的认知。
设计与对象
本研究分析了2026年世界杯窗口期内Reddit上关于麻疹、汉坦病毒及埃博拉病毒的主张,采用两名编码员评估这些主张的准确性与方向性。
核心方法
两名编码员评估了Reddit上关于麻疹、汉坦病毒及埃博拉病毒主张的准确性与方向性,并使用Cohen's kappa衡量编码员间一致性。
主要结果
在66条编码主张中,41条不准确,其中24条低估风险,17条夸大风险。麻疹获得的关注度显著低于汉坦病毒,前者仅收到8条主张(66条中的8条)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,2026年世界杯窗口期内公众话语并未与实际疾病风险保持一致,麻疹尽管是上升威胁却获得的关注度低于汉坦病毒。
科研与公卫意义
本研究强调了监测在线话语以更好理解并应对公众对疾病风险认知的重要性,特别是在大型活动期间针对麻疹的风险。
局限与证据强度
由于精选的单平台样本,本研究的模式仅具探索性,但它们表明针对恐慌的防护措施可能忽略对真实威胁的否认,这可能影响公共卫生结局。

Reading the Infodemic Against the Epidemic: A Real-Risk Benchmark for Inflated and Downplayed Disease Risk on Reddit Within the 2026 World Cup Window

Authors: Lulu Alarfaj, Nimisha Rawat, Melissa Zeynep Ertem
Original abstract (English)
During the deadly hantavirus outbreak of May 2026, the loudest online response called it a hoax, while measles, the one rising threat, drew little attention. That inversion is the half of the infodemic we rarely measure: not amplified fear, but the quiet downplaying of a real risk. Detecting it requires what the field lacks: an external benchmark for the true risk. Anchoring one in the epidemiological and mass-gathering literature, with the 2026 FIFA World Cup run-up as a dated window, two coders judged Reddit claims about measles (high), hantavirus (low), and Ebola (near zero) for accuracy and direction (Cohen's kappa 0.72 and 0.68). Of 66 coded claims, 41 were inaccurate, and distortion ran both ways: 24 downplayed risk and 17 inflated it (exact binomial p = 0.35), with downplaying at least as common as inflating. Attention ran opposite to danger, hantavirus drawing the most claims (44 of 66) and measles the fewest (8): the infodemic did not mirror the epidemic. The curated, single-platform sample makes these patterns exploratory, but they suggest that defenses built against panic alone miss its mirror image, the denial of a real threat that prior work links to weaker protection among the most exposed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the discrepancy between public attention and actual disease risk during the 2026 hantavirus outbreak, highlighting the need to understand how online discourse distorts perceptions of real threats like measles.
Design and population
The study analyzed Reddit claims about measles, hantavirus, and Ebola during the 2026 World Cup window, using two coders to assess the accuracy and direction of these claims.
Core methods
Two coders evaluated the accuracy and direction of claims about measles, hantavirus, and Ebola on Reddit, using Cohen's kappa to measure intercoder agreement.
Main results
Of 66 coded claims, 41 were inaccurate, with 24 downplaying risk and 17 inflating it. Attention to measles was significantly lower than to hantavirus, with measles receiving the fewest claims (8 of 66).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that public discourse during the 2026 World Cup window did not align with actual disease risk, with measles receiving less attention than hantavirus despite being a rising threat.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights the importance of monitoring online discourse to better understand and address public perception of disease risks, particularly for measles during large-scale events.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's patterns are exploratory due to the curated, single-platform sample, but they suggest that defenses against panic may overlook the denial of real threats, which could impact public health outcomes.

灭活病毒培养物中尼帕病毒核衣壳抗原的快速检测

A rapid detection of Nipah virus nucleocapsid antigen in inactivated viral cultures.
作者： Gyeongmu Choi, Jung Kim, Hansaem Lee, Pyeonghwa Jeon, Jae Hun Jeong, Yujin Jung, Jin Seo Park, Mi Kyoung Lee, Kyung-Chang Kim, Jong-Hwan Lee
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尼帕病毒(NiV)是一种优先病原体,可引起严重呼吸系统疾病和脑炎,在资源有限环境中,快速诊断对于疫情防控至关重要,而核酸检测在这些环境中往往不切实际。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种针对 NiV 的快速抗原测试,使用针对病毒核衣壳蛋白的纤维素纳米珠增强侧流免疫测定法。
核心方法
从人工合成噬菌体展示文库中分离出抗NiV核衣壳蛋白抗体,将其重组为IgG,并通过ELISA和免疫荧光法进行筛选,随后在夹心配置中进行抗体配对以用于检测。
主要结果
优化后的CNB-LFIA可检测重组NiV NP至1 ng/mL,对SARS-CoV-2、SARS-CoV、MERS-CoV或流感A病毒无交叉反应,并可检测γ射线灭活的NiV培养物至4.1 × 10^7拷贝/mL(读取器检测)或8.3 × 10^7拷贝/mL(肉眼检测)。
解释与创新
作者将该测定解释为提供快速、无需设备的 NiV 检测,适合在 暴发 多发环境中进行即时护理,强调了其在分子诊断不可用的情况下的潜在用途。
科研与公卫意义
该CNB-LFIA有望在资源有限环境中实现及时的NiV 诊断,支持疫情防控与患者管理,适用于实验室基础设施有限的地区。
局限与证据强度
现有证据仅限于分析性能和培养病毒检测;临床验证、现场性能及与其他亨尼帕病毒的交叉反应尚未评估,证据强度为中等。

A rapid detection of Nipah virus nucleocapsid antigen in inactivated viral cultures.

Authors: Gyeongmu Choi, Jung Kim, Hansaem Lee, Pyeonghwa Jeon, Jae Hun Jeong, Yujin Jung, Jin Seo Park, Mi Kyoung Lee, Kyung-Chang Kim, Jong-Hwan Lee
Original abstract (English)
Nipah virus (NiV) is a highly lethal zoonotic henipavirus that causes severe respiratory disease and encephalitis and remains a priority pathogen for outbreak preparedness. Timely diagnosis is essential for containment, yet nucleic acid-based testing is often impractical in outbreak-prone, resource-limited settings. Here, we report the development of a rapid antigen test for NiV based on a cellulose nanobead (CNB)-enhanced lateral flow immunoassay (LFIA) targeting the viral nucleocapsid protein (NP). Anti-NiV NP antibodies were isolated from human synthetic phage-display libraries (scFv and Fab) by iterative bio-panning, reformatted as full-length IgGs, and screened using ELISA and immunofluorescence assays. Four lead antibodies were advanced to a sandwich-pairing matrix, which identified an optimized capture-detection configuration (mAb #5 as capture; mAb #2 as detection). Bio-layer interferometry confirmed nanomolar binding affinities for the selected antibodies (K D = 39.50 nM for mAb #5; K D = 26.36 nM for mAb #2). The finalized CNB-LFIA produced results within 15 min and achieved an analytical limit of detection of 1 ng/mL for recombinant NiV NP. The assay observed no cross-reactivity with SARS-CoV-2, SARS-CoV, MERS-CoV, or influenza A, whereas weak cross-reactivity for measles virus NP was observed only at a high antigen concentration (2 μg/mL). Using γ-irradiated cultured NiV quantified by RT-ddPCR, the LFIA detected viral load down to 4.1 × 10 7 copies/mL (4.1 × 106 copies/reaction) by a portable reader and 8.3 × 10 7 copies/mL (8.3 × 106 copies/reaction) by the naked eye. Collectively, this CNB-LFIA provides rapid, equipment-free NiV detection, supporting its potential utility for point-of-care diagnosis in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Nipah virus (NiV) is a priority pathogen causing severe respiratory disease and encephalitis, and rapid diagnosis is critical for outbreak control in resource-limited settings where nucleic acid testing is impractical.
Design and population
The study developed a rapid antigen test for NiV using a cellulose nanobead-enhanced lateral flow immunoassay targeting the viral nucleocapsid protein.
Core methods
Anti-NiV nucleocapsid protein antibodies were isolated from human synthetic phage-display libraries, reformatted as IgGs, screened by ELISA and immunofluorescence, and paired in a sandwich configuration for the assay.
Main results
The optimized CNB-LFIA detected recombinant NiV NP down to 1 ng/mL, showed no cross-reactivity with SARS-CoV-2, SARS-CoV, MERS-CoV, or influenza A, and detected γ-irradiated cultured NiV at 4.1 × 10^7 copies/mL by reader or 8.3 × 10^7 copies/mL by naked eye.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the assay as providing rapid, equipment-free NiV detection suitable for point-of-care use in outbreak-prone settings, highlighting its potential utility where molecular diagnostics are unavailable.
Scientific and public-health significance
This CNB-LFIA could enable timely NiV diagnosis in resource-limited settings, supporting outbreak containment and patient management where laboratory infrastructure is limited.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to analytical performance and cultured virus detection; clinical validation, field performance, and cross-reactivity with other henipaviruses remain unassessed, warranting a moderate confidence rating.

依赖他人免疫

Dependent on the immunity of others
作者： Pascal Muijtjens, Kevin Meesters, Muijtjens P, Meesters K.
摘要中文翻译帕斯卡尔·缪伊滕斯 我的儿子马克斯是一名28岁的医学生,于2025年因麻疹去世。四年前,他因急性白血病接受治疗,先是化疗,后接受其兄弟捐赠的骨髓移植。癌症复发后,嵌合抗原受体T细胞疗法———种通过重新设计患者自身免疫细胞以对抗癌症的免疫治疗方法———再次将他从病魔手中夺回。当时,他正在攻读健康与生命科学学士学位,这些经历塑造了他的使命感。他决定成为一名肿瘤科医生,帮助他人战胜他曾经承受的痛苦。在住院期间,他通过了竞争激烈的阿姆斯特丹四年制医学项目选拔。到医学院第三年时,马克斯的状态越来越好。我看到他带着好奇与热情在医院轮转,从与患者接触中汲取力量,探索医学各专科的广度。看到他多年治疗与不确定性后重新穿上白大褂,我心中充满自豪与宽慰。我深深钦佩他将白血病康复转化为他人力量源泉的方式,用自己的经历支持面临健康挑战的患者。马克斯因移植物抗宿主病而长期服用免疫抑制剂,并在临床轮转前接受了大量感染预防与控制咨询,包括医护相关传播的风险。为重拾生活与医学培训,他严格遵循这些预防措施。作为父亲,我感受到想要保护他免受一切可能风险的渴望与鼓励他重新夺回努力拼搏的生活之间的矛盾。他已重...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究强调了免疫功能低下个体感染麻疹的风险及麻疹疫苗接种率下降对公共卫生的更广泛影响,强调群体免疫的重要性。
设计与对象
本研究为一份病例报告,详述了一名28岁医学生因白血病接受骨髓移植后因移植物抗宿主病免疫抑制而感染麻疹后死亡的经历。
核心方法
该病例通过个人叙述、医疗记录及公共卫生调查进行记录,其中包括检测以确认麻疹为感染原因,以及尸检以确定死亡原因。
主要结果
患者出现呼吸窘迫并被诊断为麻疹,进而发展为弥漫性肺炎,并于三周后死亡。公共卫生部门无法追踪感染源。
解释与创新
作者将马克斯的死亡解读为麻疹疫苗接种率下降如何危及依赖群体免疫个体的悲剧案例,并强调此类选择的个人与职业影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果凸显了麻疹疫苗接种在保护免疫功能低下个体中的关键作用,并强调公共卫生信息传递需以同理心与证据为基础应对疫苗接种犹豫。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于单一病例且缺乏统计分析,但为麻疹疫苗接种覆盖率低下后果提供了引人注目的真实案例,支持中等信心评估。

Dependent on the immunity of others

Authors: Pascal Muijtjens, Kevin Meesters, Muijtjens P, Meesters K.
Original abstract (English)
Pascal Muijtjens My son Max was a 28-year-old medical student when he died of measles in 2025. Four years earlier, he had been treated for acute leukemia, first with chemotherapy, then with a bone marrow donation from his brother. When the cancer returned, chimeric antigen receptor T-cell therapy — a form of immunotherapy that re-engineers a patient’s own immune cells to fight cancer — brought him back once more. At the time, he was doing a bachelor’s degree in health and life sciences, and these experiences shaped his sense of purpose. He decided he wanted to become an oncologist, to help others fight what he had endured. During his hospital admissions, he passed the highly competitive selection process for the four-year medical program in Amsterdam. By his third year of medical school, Max was thriving. I watched him rotate through hospitals with curiosity and enthusiasm, drawing energy from his contact with patients and from exploring the breadth of medical specialties. Seeing him wear his white coat after years of treatment and uncertainty filled me with pride and relief. I deeply admired the way he transformed his own recovery from leukemia into a source of strength for others, using his experience to support patients facing their own health challenges. Max remained on immunosuppressive medication for graft-versus-host-disease and, in preparation for his clinical rotations, he had been counselled extensively on infection prevention and control, including the risks of health care–associated transmission. Determined to resume his life and medical training, he followed these precautions carefully. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study highlights the risk of measles for immunocompromised individuals and the broader public health implications of declining vaccination rates, emphasizing the importance of community immunity.
Design and population
The study is a narrative case report detailing the experience of a 28-year-old medical student who died from measles after receiving a bone marrow transplant for leukemia and being immunocompromised due to graft-versus-host-disease medication.
Core methods
The case was documented through personal accounts, medical records, and public health investigations, which included testing to confirm measles as the cause of infection and an autopsy to determine the cause of death.
Main results
The patient developed respiratory distress and was diagnosed with measles, leading to diffuse pneumonia and ultimately his death three weeks later. Public health authorities could not trace the source of the infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret Max's death as a tragic example of how declining vaccination rates can endanger individuals who rely on herd immunity, and they highlight the personal and professional impact of such choices.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the critical role of vaccination in protecting immunocompromised individuals and emphasize the need for public health messaging that addresses vaccine hesitancy with empathy and evidence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a single case and lacks statistical analysis, but it provides a compelling, real-world example of the consequences of low vaccination coverage, supporting a moderate confidence assessment.
